Zero MuzzlePoint is the muzzle origin by construction, and the envelope ends at muzzle+0.03 (GunCollider.cs:511), as in the analysed path. Only the root case (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:266-267) makes zero mean the root, which is the game's own firing origin (Weapon.cs:1560). The proposed fix would put the muzzle 0.8 m past the real one. Better fix: No fix for this. Do not apply d.MuzzlePoint = GripPoint + AimAxis * 0.8: it moves the muzzle 0.8 m into empty space and enlarges the envelope and every MuzzlePoint consumer. Optional only: in the root case a geometric muzzle host (as WeaponAnalyzer.cs:436-445 does) could be used, but that changes GunBody and is a design change, not this bug.
- [-] 251.32 [refuted, tiny, helper] src/UI/SquadPointing.cs:189: Squad order label and aim dereference a missing SquadPointUi during its fade-out.
  What: open is false when ui == null (line 168), but show stays true while _alpha fades (169-170). While Pointing is true the code still reads ui.text (line 189: Pointing ? (ui.text != null ? ui.text.text : "GO TO") ...) and calls Target(ui) at line 198, which reads ui.aimPoint (line 218).
  Skeptic (not real): Not real. SquadPointUi.instance (.ref/decompiled/Assembly-CSharp/SquadPointUi.cs:24) is set only in Awake (:166) and never nulled, so after a scene change ui is a destroyed MonoBehaviour, not C# null. Unity != null makes open false (SquadPointing.cs:168), but ui.text, ui.aimPoint and ui.aimActor (189, 218-224) are plain managed field reads that do not throw. Target's native calls are guarded by Unity null checks. No NRE path.
  Skeptic (not real): SquadPointUi.instance (.ref/decompiled/Assembly-CSharp/SquadPointUi.cs:24) is set only in Awake (:166) and never nulled; no SquadPointUi OnDestroy exists. After the UI is destroyed, ui is a non-null managed reference, so ui != null (SquadPointing.cs:168) is false by Unity's overload, the fade runs, and ui.text (:56), ui.aimPoint (:54), ui.aimActor (:52) are plain field reads that do not throw. The guard at :189 short-circuits on ui.text != null. Only a cosmetic label/arrow fallback results, no exception. Better fix: None needed. The finding conflates Unity's destroyed-object null check with C# null: the reads at lines 189 and 198 do not throw on a destroyed SquadPointUi, so the code is safe as written.

### For 252: what the read-only Haiku scouts found (2026-10-16 night) and what was done

- (m) Seated magazine jumping to the hand: PullSeatedMagazine put it on the rail with RailLateral0 = 0, and the rail's 7 cm off-line test knocked it off on the first frame (the grab zone is ~10 cm), then it blended straight to the hand through the receiver. Fixed: the grab's lateral offset is the baseline, and a magazine still in its well stays on the rail up to 20 cm off-line (it leaves only out of the mouth).
- (j) Spare magazine behind pistols: no pistol still from weapons-1016c shows one (the stills are from the front). Likeliest cause: CollectSpareMagazines only takes spares resting 28 cm or more from the grip; a spare held just behind the pistol stays drawn. Done: 12 cm for handguns. Unsure stills to check: weapon-062-GLOCK_43X-S (dark oval left of the pistol), weapon-075-vector / 080-mwii-g17 / 106-apc45 (a slab bottom right). Other possible causes: on the bore line within 6 cm, the caps (4 spares, 8 props), skinned ARS guns (other magazine bones hidden only within 3 cm).
- (k) MK18RII light and laser backwards: nothing in the mod or the game turns them round; most likely the emitter in the asset faces back (mk18riii is in no rig dump; its .rfc is unreadable here). Done: an emitter (LaserSight or spot Light) more than 120 degrees off the bore is turned round every frame (logged once). Same family to watch: light_mk18_acog (056), light_mk18_EXPS3 (057); HK416-B's Laser Sight under HK416控制器.
- (e) M1 GARRET: the pouch's "empty clip" was the emptied clip lying by the hip: a dropped magazine within 13 cm of the palm beat the pouch, whatever its rounds (any gun). Fixed: an empty one is never taken with the palm in the pouch. Still to do (218, en-bloc mode): it locks open only if analysed SelfLoading with a slide (a Manual one never locks: OnShot locks only when CyclesItself); its clip axis is (0, -0.70, -0.72) so the clip goes in up and forward instead of down from the top (MagAxisFromShape assumes a magazine below the bore); nothing ejects the clip after the last shot and no ping cue is bound (MechSound has none; find the game's ping in its SoundBank / reload cues); the bolt should close on its own when a clip is seated.
- (g) Hands and magazines through guns, ranked: 1. the glove's keep-out was eased in (28% a frame), so a fast shove left it ~7 cm inside the gun: fixed, pushed out at once and eased back only; 2. the free hand's glove is checked a frame late and ShortestWayOut can exit on the far side (to do: sweep from its last clear place, exit against its motion); 3. a magazine 30 cm behind the hand jumped to it through the gun: fixed, it stays outside and is let go 45 cm on; 4. the magwell was open 1.5 cm a side: fixed (4 mm); 5. off the well line the magazine hand's fingers do not close on the gun (to do); 6. the freed-from-inside search can exit the far side (to do); 7. no keep-out until the solid is measured (to do: fall back to the envelope box); 8. parts outside the collider envelope (long suppressors, wide rails on handguns) have no solid (to do); 9-10. fingers starting inside, the free hand's fingers get no closure (to do).
- (h) Magazines going in through the gun on many guns (pistols, HK416-B/D, CZ Bren 2 BR): the HK416-B's 30% picture shows the magazine 2 cm back toward the trigger guard: its line is the reload animation's pull, 18 degrees toward the grip (MagAxisFromShape trusts the pull over an unreadable shape's box within 20 degrees). Done: only on handguns now; a long gun's box line is used. Also the well opening (4 mm). Next: fit each gun's magazine line to its measured solid once it is measured (candidates +-24 degrees in the side plane, the least of the magazine passing through the gun outside its well).

- (w) Night vision: it is the game's own goggles (GameManager.defaultNightVisionGoggles, toggled by the off-hand grip beside the head, BodyZones 209-214 -> KeyBinds.Goggles). Its image effect (GenericImageEffect OnRenderImage, or the NV post-process profile) runs on the flat game camera only (CameraSuppressor sets those cameras to draw to the desktop only); the eyes get only the grey actor glow and the NV canvas turned into a 72-degree HUD frame. To do: our own night-vision pass on the eye camera (OnRenderImage on VRRig.Camera while NightVision.isEnabled: luminance gain, green tint, grain, vignette), or copy the game's GenericImageEffect material; hide the game's grey vignette in VR meanwhile.
- (p) Two weapons at once: nothing exists yet beyond 152's hand swap. The mod knows one held weapon (WeaponSystem.Held) and the game one activeWeapon; taking another weapon goes through TakeIntoHand -> actor.SwitchWeapon, which holsters the first. Design (staged): a second HeldWeapon bound to the off hand (WeaponSystem.Second; HeldWeapon parameterised by its gun and support hands), shown without being the game's active weapon (the slot trick at WeaponDrops 1124), fired with the off hand's own trigger through Weapon.Shoot (semi latched, auto on the cooldown), its own AmmoState; pairs by Weapon.slot / type (primary 0, secondary 1, launcher = LargeGear 3, grenade = ThrowableWeapon; never two primaries); per-hand SuppressFire; one GunCollider per held weapon; letting go of the second never holsters the main one; reloading either while both hands are full is blocked in v1 (holster one first).
- Support hand trigger: the left trigger is the game's Use (not Fire). On a gun it now works the pump, fires an under-barrel launcher (switched to while held), or steps the scripts' laser / light keys; while the support hand holds such a gun, that trigger is not passed to the game as Use.

- (u) Shotguns reloadable: in the last full run every shotgun loaded full by hand and fired (R870 BUCKSHOT/SLUG 8/8, R870 CQC 6/6, mw-m870 8/8, M1014 and Tactical 8/8, ARS M500 and Bot Friendly 7/7; DOUBLE-BARREL, SAWED-OFF and the six M1878s 2/2). To recheck in the headset with the pump now on the support hand's trigger.
- (v) Break-action shells: Casings.Drop returned when the gun had no flat casing particles (the vanilla DOUBLE-BARREL and SAWED-OFF), after the count was already taken: fixed, an opened break action always lets its fired cases out. Still to do: the shell in the hand and the ones thrown out are the mod's 12-gauge lathe, not the gun's own model (CollectReloadRounds skips parts hidden at rest and never uses RoundName; a shell part could become MagazinePath before the hinge is found): copy the gun's shell part (a new ShellPath) for the hand shell, the dropped ones and the chamber; one "shell" rule for KindOf and ShotgunShells (a revolver's .357 is a red shell in the hand, brass when dropped).

- (n) Machine gun boxes: no belt-fed gun has an in-hand test picture (MagazineDeepCheck skips a box locked under a shut cover, and "pulled out by hand: stays in the hand" only checks a prop exists). Likeliest cause of both symptoms: a belt part wins the magazine pick (MagName has belt / ammo / 弹链, weight 3, while a box named 弹箱 scored 1): a long belt is "only the belt comes out", a link a few centimetres long is "only the number shows". Done: on a belt-fed gun (40+ rounds, automatic) a belt piece (BeltName, or a plain "ammo"/"AMMO.001" part) is never the box (under 6 cm skipped, otherwise weight 0.5); ammo box / ammo can / box mag / 弹箱 count as magazine names. To do: log the chosen magazine part and copy-or-stand-in at the first eject; let the deep check open the cover and take the box (and check something visible is in the hand); MK46 ET's analysed box is 65 cm against a 31 cm copy (belt start from the wrong height); the tall black block at mg3's feed end (weapon-153).
- (ac) The mwii M4s' charging handle cannot be cut out (their meshes are unreadable) and no shader can clip it at draw time without a shader bundle built in a Unity 2020.3 editor (none on this PC). Done: the added piece on an AR-pattern gun is a slim T latch, not a cube.

- (e) M1 GARRET en-bloc mode coded (untested): by name (garand / garret), the clip goes in from the top only with the action open, seating it sends the bolt home, the last shot throws the empty clip up with the gun's own ping (its "Bank Audio Source", garand_clip) and the bolt stays open.

The fallback skips any mesh for which GunCollider.SeeThrough is true. That is true for renderQueue > 2500 (GunCollider.cs:625), not only for glow, lens or laser parts. A gun body on a transparent or glass-like material is therefore never the host, and the fallback picks a smaller opaque part or returns null, so gunBody is wrong for that gun. For the laid state, build the strip once in gun space and parent it to the gun body, as the finding suggests, if the tolerance is not enough.

- [x] 255.I1-4 (improvement, tiny, skeptic medium) src/Weapons/ReloadInteractions.cs:1344: Rail capture and release share one -3 cm boundary (no hysteresis). Each capture replays the lip click (lines 1365-1367) and haptic (line 1364) and restarts the 80 ms switch blend (MagazineProp.OnRail setter, MagazineProp.cs 101-115). Trigger: A magazine pulled to the lip and held still there, with the hand jittering a few millimetres across the -3 cm line. Fix: For magazines only (rockets keep their own band), capture only when along > -0.005f, and keep the release at -0.03f. That leaves a 2.5 cm band where the state does not flip. Leave the rocket condition unchanged.
